# Wikipedia Attribution

Game descriptions in gamedb.txt are adapted from the opening sentences of the following English Wikipedia articles, by their respective Wikipedia contributors, under [CC BY-SA 4.0](https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by-sa/4.0/). The complete license is in licenses/CC-BY-SA-4.0.txt. Each history link identifies the contributors.

Changes: short introduction excerpts, ASCII punctuation and accents, removal of pronunciations, romanisations and foreign-script titles, and omission of disambiguation and list pages. These adapted descriptions remain under CC BY-SA 4.0. For 2.0 the descriptions of NES, WonderSwan, Genesis / Mega Drive, Master System, Game Gear, SG-1000 and Sega CD games were added from the same source: the English Wikipedia introductions of the articles that Wikidata (CC0) lists as released for those consoles (platform property P400; items Q172742, Q1065792, Q1048035, Q10676, Q209868, Q751719, Q1136956 and Q1047516), fetched on 21 November 2026 through the Wikimedia API (TextExtracts, plain text, at most three sentences, then cut to whole sentences within about 200 characters). Game titles are matched to articles by the No-Intro / Redump names of libretro-database revision `7d716259` (catalogue version 2026.11.14). The list below has one entry for every article whose text is in the game list. No endorsement is implied. Wikidata title/article mappings are CC0. The source export did not preserve individual Wikipedia revision IDs; these links identify the articles and their revision histories, not a claimed exact revision.
